# 1998 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** empty Google vs packed Yahoo
**Star:** I'm Feeling Lucky · `itt98-lucky` · `sites/google/lucky.html`
**ILS June:** 2,410,067 · 188,023,930
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 404
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt98-lucky`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/1998/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/1998/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 1998 — empty search vs packed portals
2. I'm Feeling Lucky — type a query REAL
3. Google! — empty & new
4. Yahoo! — packed portal TV ads
5. Buy a CD · eBay
6. Year flow map

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

---

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt98-lucky`

**Room:** `years/1998/sites/google/lucky.html`
**Hook:** `data-google-lucky`
**Nostalgia:** I'm Feeling Lucky — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- Empty query · Yahoo-as-gold trap never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt98-lucky`.

**Complete writes**
- Type a query ≥2 · Lucky. Writes itt98-lucky.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `1998` · key `itt98-lucky`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-lucky` — do not write `itt97-*` or `itt99-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** Amazon Music → `years/1998/sites/amazon/music.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

## L · Leftover-official — every dest · 404 flows

Protocol is the same machine (`js/immersion/leftover-official.js`). The minutes below are dest-true: key, needPick, field, next, hops.

### L-0001 · `itt98-ab-lx` · sites/about/index.html

**Open:** `/years/1998/sites/about/index.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="ab-lx"])`
**Need:** field ≥2 (placeholder “about leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `yahoo` · `google` · `amazon`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="ab-lx"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt98-ab-lx']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt98-lucky` stays empty. `itt97-ab-lx` and `itt99-ab-lx` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “about leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="ab-lx"]`.
- `localStorage['itt98-ab-lx']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"1998", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt98-ab-lx`.
- Does **not** write `itt98-lucky`.
- **Next:** DMOZ leftover → `/years/1998/sites/dmoz/index.html`
